## Method

All 1,020 receipts were checked against the classified inventory (review-doc-inventory: 818 duplicate and 218 semantic groups): key present; evidence 'Current members' list equals the inventory members; member-set sha256 recomputed (815 of 815 duplicate groups match); for duplicates every member's git blob equals the group blob (verified at HEAD), the named owner is a member under docs/platform/agent-coordination, and all members lie under the two area roots; for semantic groups the resolution text was compared with the group's real shape (same-relative architect/platform pair, or distinct-scope proof artifacts) and blobs were compared. The receipt that 'the independent reviewer must inspect 20 sampled evidence-name groups' is met by a reviewer draw (below). The three duplicate groups and 13 semantic groups the frozen retirement leaves outside this batch (two docs/history iron-law pairs, the empty-blob group, and 13 docs/history name groups) were not in scope and are not approved here.

## Result

- Receipts: 1,020; ok 950; rework 70; hold 0.
- Duplicate (815): all ok. Owner is always a platform member; no group mixes areas; 11 groups have more than two members (up to 6) and were read for owner choice.
- Semantic (205): 135 ok (66 same-relative architect/platform preservation pairs whose blobs differ only by candidate framing, and 69 evidence-name collisions). 70 rework:
  1. 69 groups keyed platform-documentation-unmapped-candidate:* are evidence-name collisions between proof artifacts of different proof scopes (for example P01.2 versus P01.3 or P05.1), but carry the pair resolution ('Source/candidate preservation pair ... platform carrier retains the source claims') and rule ('Single mapped owner with source-row conservation'), which describe a different situation; the receipts must use the evidence-name-collision resolution already used for their architect-side twins.
  2. agent-coordination:stage-operation-taskspec-skill-relationship: the architect and platform SVG have the same blob, so 'different blob' is false; it is a plain duplicate pair.

## Evidence-name sample drawn by the reviewer

The reviewer drew 20 of the 138 evidence-name collision groups (69 architect-keyed, 69 platform-keyed) with seed 7166078e7e04baab (python3 random.Random(int(seed,16)).sample(sorted(keys), 20)) and compared the members' sizes, first lines and blobs: all 20 are genuinely distinct artifacts of different proof runs, cells or roles (distinct blobs in every group; names such as r8-live-proof-run1, close.json, driver.mjs, v3-01-open.json, 7-synthesizer-raw.log, result.json with 22 members). No group of the sample merges observations; the only defect found is the wrong resolution text on the platform-keyed ones (9 of the 20 were platform-keyed).
